connect target /
run {

#SET UNTIL TIME="TO_DATE('2021-08-09 22:41:17','yyyy-mm-dd hh24:mi:ss')";

SET NEWNAME FOR DATABASE to '/T24DEV1/DATAFILE/%b';

SET NEWNAME FOR TEMPFILE 1 TO '/T24DEV1/TEMPFILE/temp01.dbf';

RESTORE DATABASE;

SWITCH DATAFILE ALL;

SWITCH TEMPFILE 1;

RECOVER DATABASE;

}

# and updated by either Database Configuration Assistant while creating
# a database or ASM Configuration Assistant while creating ASM instance.

# A colon, ':', is used as the field terminator.  A new line terminates
